Use comptime @typeInfo(c.VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures).Struct.fields to assert exactly55 fields, each VkBool32; no struct/raw-wire cast or contiguous payload memcpy. Struct declaration order is the pinned core feature wire order. Existing recognized type assertions remain unchanged. Preserve every outer sType/pNext byte, implicit padding byte, adjacent canary and all unknown object bytes. Publication errors return Invalid and leave the complete outer object plus all recognized outputs unchanged, including the case of a malformed last chain record or final core word.

Linux/Windows x64 compile-time ABI proof: VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures size220/alignment4; VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures2 size240/alignment8, features offset16, pNext offset8. The adapter retains existing chain104B/seen512B/record196B ABI. No owned core copy is required because immutable borrowed core inputs are explicitly disjoint from outputs; validation uses bounded scalar temporaries, fixed storage only.

Required tests before the atomic increment:
- Every55 native core field one-hot with independent native @offsetOf byte expectation, A5-filled outer padding/header and adjacent canaries; native known chain outputs simultaneously verified.
- Full byte preservation on core lengths0/54/56, bad core words at every position (2 and maximal u32), malformed final chain Boolean/count/tag/header, duplicate/null/misaligned targets and incorrect outer sType/alignment.
- Zero-chain success and maximal8 known-chain success; unknown nodes before/between/after recognized outputs retain header/payload/canaries.
- Aligned initialized recognized target overlapping outer object rejected with exact pre/post byte comparison; adjacent target extent permitted, proving half-open interval boundary.
- Native tests and independently instrumented C-oracle ASan/LSan/UBSan run, x64 Windows compile with ABI assertions, production coverage>=90% line/branch. Source fixture boundary marker stays before all test-only helpers.

No public Features2 procedure is installed and no API or extension is advertised by this prerequisite. ICD integration must establish actual raw host/schema support independently and must not publish core or chain separately before this whole-operation validator succeeds.

### Owned negotiated capability binding for modern query prerequisites

Add venus_icd_bind_capabilities(exchange, context, capabilities) as a documented non-Vulkan C export alongside unchanged legacy venus_icd_bind. Each nonnull input is borrowed for the synchronous bind validation, except callback/context which remain borrowed until unbind/abandon under the existing exclusive receiver ownership contract. capabilities points to one initialized immutable caller-owned160-byte venus_capabilities_t. Validate all three pointers, unoccupied binding, exact venus_capabilities_compatible profile and namespace availability before publishing any object namespace or callback. Rejected null/incompatible/occupied/exhausted binds leave the active namespace, callback, command state and copied capability record unchanged. No host transaction occurs during binding. Caller synchronization prevents capability mutations during copy; no pointer to the input record is retained.

The ICD owns one zero-initialized copied160-byte capability record and Boolean readiness flag under its existing global mutex. A private bind helper assumes that mutex is held and initializes existing object/command ownership exactly once; public bind functions never call another locking entry point. Legacy successful binding explicitly clears capability storage/readiness and remains available for existing core1.0 tests and clients. Additive successful binding publishes the copied record only after the existing object/command initialization succeeds. Caller may immediately mutate/free its input after return without affecting negotiation. clear(), successful quiescent unbind and receiver-retired abandon scrub the record/readiness. Failed unbind retains it with the live session. Compile-time sizeof/align checks remain the existing160-byte integer ABI; measure exact ready-flag storage separately rather than silently inflating the previous fixed metadata ledger.

Export the additive symbol in both ELF map and Windows DEF, include venus_capabilities.h in the public ICD header, and link the already owned capability codec into all native/shared/Windows/coverage ICD targets. Production-worker direct and dynamically loaded dispatch paths bind the actual previously negotiated guest.capabilities, with loader symbol resolution failing closed if absent. Existing receiver callback, channel, mappings and workers retain their current retirement ordering. No parser bit is hardware support and this additive binding alone installs no new Vulkan query, enables no feature, and raises neither API nor extension advertisement. The Windows TCP bootstrap must eventually use its authenticated actual captured capability record through this same boundary.

Tests before source commit: every null parameter; incompatible version/Boolean/mask/profile; occupied legacy and negotiated binds preserving prior callback/storage; namespace exhaustion preserving output; successful caller-record mutation proves deep copy; successful/failed unbind and abandon storage lifecycle; a legacy rebind after negotiated teardown must leave zero readiness/storage. Native shared-library symbol lookup and Windows exports must resolve the exact C ABI. Actual production-worker direct/shared gates must pass the genuine negotiated record, including normal/sanitized lifecycle checks with zero bytes leaked. Require full current native dispatch/coverage>=90% and Windows crosscompile. This prerequisite receives no modern Vulkan/DXVK completion credit by itself.

##### Owned ICD ASan gate: compiler interoperability contract

Required additional Linux x64 gate: scripts/icd_owned_sanitizers.py. Its artifact directory is exclusive and separate from normal/shared/physical build outputs. No production source, suppression file or compiler panic is rewritten.

#### Current gap and exact scope

`build/venus_icd.o` currently compiles Zig0.13.0 ReleaseSafe without ASan. Linking
it into the C fixture with `-fsanitize=address,leak,undefined` instruments the C
fixture and activates process allocation/LSan interposition, but does not insert
ASan checks at the Zig object's loads/stores. `nm -u build/venus_icd.o` shows no
ASan/UBSan access hooks. Existing graphics-state LLVM tooling instruments only
that selected module. Clang's UBSan frontend pass does not retroactively generate
C-language UB checks from Zig LLVM IR; this proposal must not claim otherwise.

The gate instruments every emitted production function whose
DISubprogram belongs to the exact requested owned Zig filename and whose canonical
name occurs before its `// Test-only fixtures.` boundary. Generic `__anon_N`
instantiations normalize to the source name. The frozen native Debug gate found159 source function names and171 source-mapped
owned definitions; the Zig-test executable has172. Both have zero omitted names.
The earlier189-definition prototype used a broader canonical-prefix selector;
the final tool narrows selection to exact source metadata and source-defined names. It
instruments both native ICD and the same ICD embedded in the Zig test executable.
Standard library/test-runner functions are not relabeled owned. Imported codecs
retain Zig Debug checks; their independently specified gates remain necessary.

#### Why Debug IR is required

The Zig emitted ReleaseSafe LLVM IR has already coalesced distinct local stack
objects. A concrete function `format_properties` has an outer64-byte lifetime and
inner8-byte lifetime on the same `%3` alloca. ASan added after coalescing poisons
those8 bytes at the inner lifetime end despite the valid outer lifetime. Removing
lifetime hints, turning off scope checking, or suppressing the report is prohibited.
Debug emitted IR keeps separate stack objects and retains Zig null, bounds,
arithmetic, alignment and explicit runtime safety checks. Debug is the additional
unit safety gate; ordinary ReleaseSafe build, coverage, ABI and physical GPU gates
remain independent and unchanged.

#### Compiler guard normalization without erasure

Zig's emitted IR already contains both `llvm.stackprotector` and explicit volatile
canary reload/compare/failure blocks. Adding ASan late relocates the slot into the
ASan frame, but backend lowering of the preexisting intrinsic stores to the old
frame. The resulting null epilogue read was reproduced under gdb in `cache(1)`;
unmodified non-ASan LLVM roundtrip passes the full native suite.

For each accepted shape only, normalize:

```
call void @llvm.stackprotector(ptr %StackGuard, ptr %StackGuardSlot)
```

to:

```
store volatile ptr %StackGuard, ptr %StackGuardSlot, align 8
```

The transformation writes exactly the same guard value to exactly the same slot
with volatile semantics. Every original guard load, comparison, branch, panic,
`__stack_chk_fail` call, SSP attribute, module initializer and LLVM lifetime hint
remains byte-for-byte intact. Backend SSP attributes are retained and can add
normal backend protection after ASan. No original function or branch is removed.
The prototype native module contains1128 normalized compiler guard stores.

The tool rejects changed intrinsic grammar, unknown slot reload shape, missing
production functions, an empty transformation, or any preservation mismatch.
After removing only its added function ASan attribute and reversing only accepted
stores, it requires exact byte equality with the entire original LLVM module.
That equality includes all compiler panic bodies and global initializers. The
sanitized object must contain both ASan load and store report hooks.

#### Execution, ownership and diagnostics

The caller exclusively owns the selected artifact directory. Normalized IR,
objects, reports, C oracle and executables live there only. Existing pinned oracle
objects and headers are read dependencies; no protocol regeneration or shared
object replacement occurs. IR input is limited to256MiB. Each tool/test subprocess
has180-second timeout and nonzero status propagates as failure. There are no
suppression options. Required runtime options are detect_leaks=1, abort_on_error=1,
halt_on_error=1; C UBSan also halts and prints its stack trace.

Both the ordinary native C fixture's28 entry-case groups (including graphics,
readback, mapped memory and cancellation paths) and all Zig unit tests run against
the genuinely ASan-instrumented ICD. The latter includes allocator tests and the
sanitized C fixture renamed `venus_icd_native_fixture`. The tool records selected
symbols, production counts, empty omission list, accepted guard count, reverse
proof and explicit UBSan scope. A new owned source can reuse the selection and
normalization utility, but its native/oracle dependency integration requires a
separate contract; the initial CLI accepts venus_icd.zig only.

  diagnostic, original guards and lifetime markers retained.
- nm shows ASan load1/4/8/16/n and store1/4/8/16 hooks.
- Earlier isolated diagnostic controls proved a real owned `enumerate_version`
  undersized output WRITE is caught by ASan and a deliberately corrupted temporary
  guard copy reaches `__stack_chk_fail`. These were completed before the latest

- Frozen ba0983c reusable tool native128-cycle/28-case suite and all74 Debug Zig
  tests pass with ASan/LSan active, no diagnostics and exact reverse proof. Native
  report:159 source names,171 definitions,1128 guard stores. Zig-test report:
  same159 source names,172 definitions,1513 guard stores. Reports/logs: `/tmp/waddle_full_icd_safety/frozen/`
  and `/tmp/waddle_full_icd_safety/frozen.log`. No further fault variants were created.

The tracked tool must add the already owned capability codec object to both native and Zig link inputs and rerun against the completed binding source before its implementation commit. Compile C fixtures with the existing strict warning flags. Select every current ICD production function dynamically, rather than hard-coding the159-name baseline. Preserve exact whole-module reverse equality and fail closed on unknown compiler/ABI guard shape. This gate verifies owned ICD Zig memory accesses, C frontend UBSan and process leak ownership; imported and separately compiled codecs retain their independently specified safety gates and cannot be described as newly ASan-instrumented by this one-file selector.

### Immutable per-physical feature queries and conservative publication

This increment implements query/cache/publication only. The Vulkan1.0 API ceiling and empty extension lists remain. Device enablement, complete physical query families, mandatory modern feature semantics and real DXVK acceptance have separate implementation and verification gates.

#### Native pointer and output contract

Expose the core and KHR Features2 procedure names only for a live instance under a successfully copied compatible negotiated binding. Legacy binding returns null for those names and never issues command147. Keep existing physical object lookup without native-handle dereference. Entry signature uses a nullable untyped output address at the C ABI boundary: an incoming typed optional pointer must not permit optimizer removal of integer alignment checks (the native adapter's earlier ReleaseSafe typed-pointer experiment demonstrated that risk). Validate null, native Features2 alignment8 and sType FEATURES_2 before reading pNext.

Collect the same-call native chain with the existing native adapter: maximum8 recognized unique tags, maximum64 headers total, pointer/cycle checks before dereferencing a65th address; unknown records contribute only their accessible sType/pNext header. Caller exclusively owns initialized accessible chain objects, headers and links for the whole call, and all objects including unknown records are disjoint/nonoverlapping. Store recognized output pointers only in bounded call-local chain_t (104 bytes/align8); there is no global application pointer retention. The512-byte seen-address array belongs to collection only. No payload of an unknown record is read or written.

Create zero initialized owned node/core staging with exactly the collected recognized tag/count schema. Call native.validate_features2 before any cache lookup that could trigger a host transaction. This preflight verifies outer240-byte overlap, known-node pair overlap, full schemas, counts, headers and Booleans without touching output. Native-input invalidity returns from the void Vulkan function with every caller byte unchanged, no receiver command and no sticky session loss. Revalidate the projected decoded stage inside publish_features2 after obtaining the raw snapshot; publication writes only named VkBool32 fields as one transaction. Preserve headers/links, native padding, unknown payloads and adjacent canaries. Caller mutation during the call is invalid usage; no second independent chain traversal is needed.

#### Exact bounded cache ownership and ABI

Add one record per existing instance physical-handle slot, not a selected-global record and not one per logical device:

    const physical_feature_cache_t = struct {
        actual_api_version: u32 = 0,
        actual_api_ready: bool = false,
        raw_features_ready: bool = false,
        raw: features_wire.result_t = .{},
    };

    // Add within existing instance_cache_t:
    feature_caches: [MaxDevices]physical_feature_cache_t =
        [_]physical_feature_cache_t{.{}} ** MaxDevices;

All data is copied scalar storage under the existing ICD global mutex. The record owns no heap, renderer handle, application pointer or descriptor. Slots follow the existing physical[count] ordering, belong to that parent's live handle generation, and are never searched without first validating the live physical registry object. Lookup may scan the16 instance caches and their bounded16 physical prefixes (<=256 comparisons), returning the corresponding record only while mutex held. Handle domain does not wrap/recycle. A successful parent destroy clears its complete instance cache using existing entry.*=.{} only after native destruction and local releases succeed. Parent destruction refused for live devices preserves its record. Failed uncertain destruction preserves ownership until caller backend-retired abandon. clear/unbind/abandon reset every record through the existing instance cache array reset. Initialization/reuse publishes zero records before exposure. No raw/effective record aliases any application output.

Measured standalone Zig0.13.0 x64 layout using the exact wire scalar shapes: node_t196/align4; wire.result_t1792/align4; physical_feature_cache_t1800/align4 with actual_api_version offset0, raw offset4, actual_api_ready1796, raw_features_ready1797. Existing instance_cache_t144/align8 becomes28944/align8, feature_caches offset140. The256 physical records add460800 bytes; complete instance-cache array grows2304 to463104 bytes. Adopt comptime assertions for real imported types and report Windows x64 sizes as well. If compiler actual imported layout differs, fail and revise documentation before committing, rather than using casts or guessed padding. Scratch contains a bounded1792-byte raw decode,1792-byte projected result,132-byte maximum wire prefix inside the existing8192-byte writer,104-byte collected output references and32-byte requested canonical tags. Existing rx4096 can contain the maximum initialized668-byte reply. No larger reply or heap allocation is needed.

#### Query and decode sequence

On first valid negotiated core/features2 request for a live physical object, ensure actual API privately, then query the complete admissible canonical list once using existing features_wire.query(record.id,tags). Unlike the old per-call caller-tag draft, raw cache fills independently of caller order/presence. This provides one immutable actual record per physical lifetime and avoids partial readiness. Call transact(writer.bytes[0..used]); copied decoded values must outlive no borrowed rx slice. Decode with exact same canonical tag list using features_wire.decode, which checks command147, pointer-presence markers, outer tag, each requested node order/tag, terminal marker, every node Boolean and every core Boolean. Initialized maximum reply is668 bytes; extra existing renderer scratch is allowed by current decoder contract, not reclassified as an initialized feature payload.

Any decode error after a sent query (Bounds/Corrupt or impossible Invalid) makes sticky RingCorrupt and preserves caller fields and cache raw readiness/content. Any native transport error follows existing sticky-loss handling and leaves feature readiness false. Actual API may already be valid from its separate complete command6; raw readiness is independent. There is no ordinary Vulkan result field in this void query; do not invent one. After complete valid decode, copy the entire raw result then set raw_features_ready last under mutex. Subsequent calls query neither properties nor features for this cache, and app output edits never mutate the raw record.

Missing canonical tags normalize to allfalse when projecting a caller chain. Build caller-order node staging independently: every recognized requested tag has its exact fixed flag_count even if absent from raw, populated with zero values in that case. Do not trim the caller native chain or link unknown payloads into the wire request. Reordering caller outputs never changes raw canonical layout. Count-only core getter and Features2 getter share the same raw snapshot when negotiated; legacy core getter still uses private command3 decode without command147.

In the direct experimental ICD fixture, resolve the implemented Features2 core/KHR entrypoints at the owned dispatch boundary and compare their identical pointer and full native-chain publication behavior. This probes the implemented driver boundary; it does not claim legal application use of an unadvertised core1.1/KHR extension surface or general Vulkan1.1 support.

The pinned Vulkan loader gates vkGetPhysicalDeviceFeatures2KHR on enabled VK_KHR_get_physical_device_properties2 (generated/vk_loader_extensions.c:10510). The loader fixture must assert that alias lookup remains null under the existing1.0/no-extension contract. It must not invoke the loader's core1.1 Features2 procedure under an API1.0 instance. Instead, invoke the legally exposed vkGetPhysicalDeviceFeatures; the negotiated implementation privately fills the same immutable actual-host canonical command147 cache before applying its all-zero guest projection. Verify repeated core calls hit that owned raw cache and publish the identical55 projected Boolean flags; retain actual-worker command/capability evidence where the callback already observes the private protocol. Direct native-chain proof and shared-loader core-query proof are separate, explicitly named outcomes. Both still require the complete real physical triangle normal/safety lifetimes, exact pixels/fences/invalidation and deterministic teardown. Preserve the first failed shared-alias run as evidence; do not relax advertised capabilities or convert its failure into success.

### Owned modern device-create request serializer

Add src/vgpu/venus_device_wire.zig as an allocation-free typed serializer and tests/vgpu/device_wire_oracle.c as its independent pinned guest-generator oracle. Add isolated scripts/vgpu_device_wire.mk targets, integrated by root only after verification. This increment encodes accepted owned values; it does not parse application pNext pointers, select supported features/extensions, reserve objects/queues, publish device enabled state, alter dispatch or raise API/extension advertisement. Those remain a separately verified public create-device increment after query/cache ownership. No new dependency/vendor change or README edit.

Inputs are nonzero distinct translated physical/device u64 identities; borrowed queue_t slices (family_index u32 and accessible immutable f32 priority slice); borrowed extension-name byte slices without a terminal NUL; an optional borrowed55-word legacy core feature slice; and borrowed feature_node_t records containing type_tag u32, flag_count u8 and55 initialized u32 Boolean slots. All inputs live exclusively and remain immutable until encode returns. The returned render.writer_t owns its8192-byte byte array and initialized used prefix, with no retained input pointer, heap, lock or native handle. Synchronous calls on disjoint input owners are thread safe. Failure returns Invalid for invalid identity/shape/value and Limit for a fully validated packet exceeding8192; no external output or transport state changes.

Bound queues to1..16 unique family records, each1..16 finite priorities in numerical[0,1], total<=64. Preserve each exact IEEE754 bit pattern, including valid negative zero, through u32 bitcast. Invalid infinity/NaN/out-of-range/empty/duplicate families fail before serialization. Bound extension count0..32; each name length4..255 excluding NUL, prefix VK_, all bytes ASCII letters/digits/underscore, no duplicate exact names. The public caller separately requires actual supported/enabled extension intersection; this codec never derives that policy from strings or receiver parser bits. Strings serialize length+1 as u64, exact bytes plus one zero terminal, then zero padding to4-byte alignment.

Bound owned feature chain to0..9 recognized unique records in caller-selected order. Each exact initialized count is: Features2=55, aggregateVulkan11=12,Vulkan12=47,Vulkan13=15,shaderDrawParameters=1,hostQueryReset=1,transformFeedback=2,robustness2=3,maintenance5=1. Every initialized Boolean must be0/1 and every unused suffix must be zero. Reject unknown/duplicate tags, aggregate11+standalone shaderDrawParameters and aggregate12+standalone hostQueryReset in either order, consistent with device-create valid-use promotion rules. A Features2 record and nonnull legacy feature slice are mutually exclusive. Legacy feature slice, if supplied, has exactly55 Boolean words. The raw host cache/caller feature support check is deliberately separate; the serializer carries exactly requested values, never replaces them with all host-supported flags. It carries neither loader-private nor unknown native nodes.

Exact command11 packet: u32 command11,u32 replyFlag1,u64 physicalId,u64 createInfoPresent1,u32 DEVICE_CREATE_INFO; for each chain node in forward order, u64 present1,u32 typeTag; terminal u64 present0; node Boolean payloads in reverse node order; u32 flags0,u32 queueRecordCount,u64 queueArrayCount; each queue has u32 DEVICE_QUEUE_CREATE_INFO,u64 pNext0,u32 flags0,u32 family,u32 priorityCount,u64 priorityArrayCount and raw f32 words; u32 layerCount0,u64 layerArray0; u32 extensionCount,u64 extensionArrayCount followed by each aligned name; u64 legacyFeaturesPresent and optional55 words; u64 allocatorPresent0,u64 deviceOutputPresent1,u64 deviceId. All integers are little endian; no native pointer, padding or struct cast is wire data. The generated VkDeviceCreateInfo pNext recursion independently confirms reverse Boolean payload order.

Preflight the complete packet extent before the first append. Base108 bytes includes fixed outer/null/array/output fields and terminal chain marker. Add32 bytes per queue record plus4 per priority;12 bytes plus4*flag_count per feature node;8 bytes plus round_up(name.len+1,4) per extension;220 bytes for present legacy features. Quotas bound all arithmetic below1048576, so usize overflow cannot occur. Valid values whose complete extent exceeds8192 return Limit before a writer exists. Native generator test output is independently8192-bound and called only for already-valid fitting fixtures. Checked writer appends remain active; full-extent proof precedes any catch-unreachable capacity assertion. Returned unused byte suffix is not part of the protocol.

The oracle accepts a canonical native VkDeviceCreateInfo with borrowed accessible typed nodes, priorities and C strings, plus translated physical/device identities and exclusive8192-byte output. It calls the pinned vn_encode_vkCreateDevice (immutable existing protocol pin7157163d5fed65f0742542a8e77e5412dea6a4bc) with replyFlag1 and null allocator; no production Zig writer or copied packet is used as expected bytes. Test construction uses named native VkBool32 members, asserts exact counts, preserves native chain order and supplies real terminators. Isolate upstream submit-stub name per translation unit. Strict C warning flags remain active with only existing vendor-header diagnostic boundaries. Native x86_64 and Windows x86_64 C/IEEE layout assertions must pass.

Meaningful tests require exact generated byte equality for null/present legacy55 one-hot flags, each9 recognized node and each initialized field one-hot, mixed/reversed legal chains with Features2 at multiple positions, different64-bit identities,16 unique queue families/64 priorities, boundary floats, empty and multiple/aligned/255-byte names. Require every invalid shape/Boolean/suffix/promotion conflict/identity/name/count/priority rejection and exact capacity below/equal/above8192 with preserved inputs, plus a repeated allocator-owned corpus with deterministic free. No fake all-supported hardware profile. Run Debug/ReleaseSafe units, fresh oracle-only C ASan/LSan/UBSan plus genuine owned Debug LLVM ASan access instrumentation retaining all compiler defenses and zero leaks, production-only branch/line coverage>=90, and Windows GNU crosscompile. Actual public device creation, native Windows/GPU behavior and DXVK remain pending until separately integrated and exercised. The reusable scripts/device_wire_sanitizers.py accepts one exclusive output base and creates a never-reused timestamp/PID run directory, preserving every prior result. It imports unchanged owned instrumentation/provenance helpers read-only with bytecode disabled. Require exactly device_wire and render_wire source inventories; preprocess/hash both fresh C oracles and all nonsystem headers, and hash scripts/source/object/executable before and after execution. ### Owned device-request preflight and immutable enablement

Root adopted this contract before implementation; preserve shared runtime/VM artifacts. Baseline: a7c9482 query/cache, abe9110 physical fixture, e1b6d27 device-wire contract and committed 06e9eee serializer; DXVK is pinned 2.7.1/c3dd74be6baec53786d4e064a572185b70347a17. “DXVK 1.3” here means this pinned DXVK's Vulkan 1.3 requirement, not an old DXVK 1.3 release. Current advertised API remains 1.0 and device/instance extension lists remain empty. Current verified optional-feature allowlists are all zero.

#### Current concrete gap

venus_icd.zig:create_device reserves a device before encode_device validates input. encode_device rejects layers/extensions/non-loader chains but accepts any 55 legacy Boolean flags without checking published support. An invalid request can consume namespace identities despite later release; a requested true flag can reach the host although both getters advertise false. New preflight must precede reservations, ring mutation and command 11. The typed owned serializer remains root-owned and independent of native parsing, support policy and lifecycle.

#### Ownership and immutable device state

Add a private device_enabled_state_t with features:features_wire.result_t and extension_mask:u32. features.core owns 55 enabled Booleans. Its eight nodes use the canonical query tags/counts: aggregate11/12/13, shaderDrawParameters, hostQueryReset, transformFeedback, robustness2, maintenance5. Initialize every canonical node/tag/count and every flag including unused tails; a live normalized record always has count 8, with absent requested semantics represented by false. A zero/unpublished record remains .{} and count 0. The normalized vector does not retain caller chain order, source mode, pointers, names, allocator callbacks or raw host feature values.

Private Zig 0.13.0 layout measurement using exact copies of the committed feature/device/render modules verifies features_wire.result_t=1792/align4, device_wire.feature_node_t=228/align4, and device_enabled_state_t=1796/align4. Adding enabled_state to the exact current device_cache_t gives size 3080/align8, enabled_state offset 544, versus current size 1280/align8: the 16-device array grows by 28800 bytes, including alignment padding, to 49280 bytes. The same compile-time assertions pass for x86_64-windows-gnu. Evidence is /tmp/waddle_device_enabled_layout/layout.zig and windows_layout.obj; no shared ICD was rebuilt. These values apply to this precise private proposed declaration. Assert them again on the actual integrated source before committing; adding or reordering any field needs renewed measurement. No public wire/header ABI change is required. Keep this state within the existing device_cache_t, with handle/nonzero live ownership as its lifetime key. All access is under the existing global mutex; borrow cache pointers only while it remains held. Never read supported raw host flags as a substitute for enabled flags.

The call-local owned_request_t separately preserves the parsed request: families:[16]u32, counts:[16]u32, priorities:[64]f32 packed in queue order, legacy:[55]u32, nodes:[9]device_wire.feature_node_t, extension_ids:[32]u8, followed by queue_count/priority_count/node_count/extension_count:u8 and legacy_present/features2_present:bool. Defaults zero every field. Queue counts encode each family's 1..16 priorities; cumulative offsets select the 64-priority array without retaining a pointer. This declaration measures 2696/align4 on both targets. preflight_scratch_t holds that request, seen:[64]usize and header_count:u8, measuring 3216/align8 on both targets. It is exclusive stack storage for the call and owns all parsed scalar values; bounded extension-name scanning resolves names to immutable compiled registry IDs before admission. No application names or pointers survive parsing. Materialize the serializer's borrowed queue/name slices only from this owned scratch and immutable registry; wrapper pointers live only for synchronous encoding. Count parsed nodes before selection, then compact accepted nodes in place. No heap allocation is required. The 8192-byte writer and staged device cache are separate existing call-local owners and are not hidden inside this scratch bound.

Publish enabled_state with the existing complete staged device cache only after command 11's exact command/identity/result validation succeeds. Ordinary explicit host failure releases all staged device/queue/ring reservations and leaves the free cache/enable-state zero. Malformed/uncertain reply or transport loss follows existing sticky binding loss: output stays NULL, reservations remain globally owned until exact backend retirement/abandon, and no live enabled state is published. Do not “undo” a possibly accepted host create. A refused/failed device teardown preserves live enabled state; acknowledged destruction scrubs it with the whole cache. clear/unbind/abandon scrub all 16 records. Do not add a second copy of enabled state for uncertainty; binding loss already prevents subsequent runtime use and the global registry/ring owners retain opaque reservations until abandonment.

#### Native input and complete structural preflight

Preserve the public VkDeviceCreateInfo ABI but enter the untrusted parser through a nullable untyped address so integer alignment checks cannot be optimized away. Check the outer pointer's integer alignment before its cast/read. Output is borrowed VkDevice storage, disjoint from every input; validate its integer alignment before its first write, and set NULL on errors when the pointer is valid/aligned. An invalid null/misaligned output returns INITIALIZATION_FAILED without dereferencing it; allocator is unused as today. Caller provides initialized accessible immutable native structs, arrays, feature objects and bounded NUL-terminated names for the call, with disjoint objects/valid pointer extents. The driver does not prove accessibility of arbitrary native addresses. It does prove bounded traversal before dereference and never retain pointers.

Validate live physical handle without dereferencing it and binding-loss status first. Then validate outer sType/flags, queues 1..16, counts 1..16,total <=64, unique families, queue headers/no unsupported queue-chain/flags, nonnull/aligned arrays and finite priorities in [0,1]; preserve float bits including negative zero. Reject enabledLayerCount>0 as LAYER_NOT_PRESENT, matching current experimental policy. Validate extension count<=32 and aligned pointer array, all required pointers, names with an accessible terminator within 256 bytes, length 4..255, exact VK_ prefix, permitted ASCII letters/digits/underscore, and exact duplicate names. String scanning is bounded; never call strlen on borrowed names.

Walk outer pNext at most 64 accessible 16-byte headers, checking integer alignment, repeated address/cycle and quota before a 65th dereference. Maintain a local 512-byte seen-address array. Skip loader-private DEVICE_CREATE_INFO headers and valid unrecognized headers; they count toward quota. Never read, copy, forward or infer Boolean semantics from unknown payloads. Unknown headers do not enable a feature or extension. Recognized supported schemas are exactly the serializer's nine types, with exact native sizes/member counts; read flags by named VkBool32 fields, not byte casts. Unknown structure tags are not “unsupported true” because no Boolean was read. Conversely, recognized extension-backed requests must undergo parser/runtime/extension admission below. Known malformed shapes/cycles/alignment and duplicate recognized tags fail structural validation.

Recognized schemas: Features2 core 55; aggregate11=12,12=47,13=15; shaderDrawParameters1;hostQueryReset1;transformFeedback2;robustness2=3;maintenance5=1. Maximum 9 recognized unique tags, preserving forward order in owned serializer input. Each flag is 0/1; zero every unused 55-word suffix. Features2 can occur anywhere in the same outer chain and supplies its embedded 55 core flags. If it exists, any nonnull pEnabledFeatures is invalid even if all zero. Otherwise copy optional pEnabledFeatures into owned core 55, defaulting to false when absent. Feature extraction reads no unknown payload. Reject aggregate11+standalone shaderDrawParameters and aggregate12+standalone hostQueryReset in either order, regardless of flag values; do not merge or resolve conflicting duplicate requests. Full shape validation precedes admission/action.

These promotion/exclusive-input rules follow the pinned type schema and Vulkan's VkDeviceCreateInfo valid-use rules; the two currently supported aggregate/standalone pairs are the only overlapping pairs in the nine-schema set. Additional promoted standalone structures must update this matrix before admission. Source: https://docs.vulkan.org/refpages/latest/refpages/source/VkDeviceCreateInfo.html . Unknown-header skipping is required by the adopted pure bounded-header semantics and never claims enablement of unimplemented payloads.

#### Policy phases and exact errors

1. Output/input/live-handle/loss checks, then complete structural snapshot: invalid null/alignment/sType/flags/quota/Boolean/duplicate/promotion/legacy-plus-Features2/name shape gives INITIALIZATION_FAILED; a layer request gives LAYER_NOT_PRESENT at the explicitly defined early layer check. No registry/cache/ring mutation or frontend call. Keep output NULL and all borrowed input bytes unchanged. Preserve each documented error's test; do not weaken an existing negative into ignored semantics except the explicit valid unknown-header rule.
2. Check runtime implementation allowlists before support lookup. Any requested true whose implementation bit is false returns FEATURE_NOT_PRESENT with zero frontend calls, no reservation, and byte-exact unchanged registry/caches/ring state. Current masks are all zero, so every true request takes this path even when the host raw cache is cold or privately true. Every requested extension name outside the compiled guest admission registry returns EXTENSION_NOT_PRESENT without a host query. Current registry is empty, so every nonempty extension request fails here. All false requests with no named extension do not require a raw feature query.
3. Under the current all-zero implementation policy, full structural validation first rejects malformed flags, duplicate/promotion conflicts and legacy-plus-Features2 exclusivity even when every flag is false. Then omit every all-false recognized modern node, including Features2 itself and all three core aggregate nodes, from the owned command 11 input. Features2's false core payload normalizes to absent legacy input; an originally provided all-false legacy pEnabledFeatures retains its owned 55-word legacy representation for compatibility. Thus accepted current requests send no modern chain, no extension names and no true optional flag. Neither parser-schema availability nor cold host API/support lookup is needed solely for these false records. Preserve caller bytes and the complete parsed source-mode markers until validation finishes. Initialize the immutable published enable-state to the canonical eight nodes with all flags false, regardless of which false records were omitted. Never silently omit any requested true: current policy rejects it in phase 2. Future positive masks retain accepted nonzero records in original relative order only after actual/public API, extension and parser admission; they do not authorize unrelated unknown payloads. Future all-false modern records still omit, avoiding schema/API dependencies with no enabled semantics.
4. Future positive implementation-mask requests can populate raw cache after complete structural/static policy validation, before any device/queue reservation. Effective support is actual raw feature support intersected with implemented semantic policy, guest advertised API/extension admission, and negotiated parser reachability. If a positive candidate's raw support is unknown, a query-only cache fill may be necessary; actually unsupported then returns FEATURE_NOT_PRESENT before reservation/command 11. Never promise zero frontend calls for unknowable actual support on a cold cache, and never impose a hidden requirement that an application queried features first. Current all zero policy avoids this phase entirely for false inputs.
5. Fully validated positive extension-backed true additionally requires an exact enabled name, an actually enumerated physical extension with sufficient specVersion, parser bit and runtime policy. Missing/unadmitted name/extension is EXTENSION_NOT_PRESENT; admitted extension with false effective Boolean is FEATURE_NOT_PRESENT. Resolve effective semantics and normalized enable-state; complete serializer extent preflight. The current selected packet has no modern nodes/names and has extent 108+32*queue_count+4*priority_count+(220 if legacy_present): at most 1096 bytes, statically below 8192 before reservations. For a future positive/extension path, root must expose a pure owned-codec size validator or an equivalently adopted capacity-proof interface before integrating the broader request; do not use fake IDs, duplicate the wire layout independently or emit a speculative create. Limit>8192 then maps to OUT_OF_HOST_MEMORY before reservations, reflecting bounded driver capacity rather than a successful partial request.
6. Only now choose a free device cache, reserve translated device/queues/rings, substitute the real translated deviceID into already-owned input and encode command 11. The validated plan's packet extent and shape must be independent of chosen identities. Encode again only on owned values: no reparse of application storage after reservation. Host explicit negative result retains its native error and releases reservations. DEVICE_LOST maps sticky loss; positive unexpected result/malformed identity maps corruption and sticky loss. ACK success publishes queues/resource ownership, enabled_state and output as one serialized local transaction.

Structural invalidity takes priority over later unsupported policy; a nonzero layer count returns LAYER_NOT_PRESENT after validating the outer header and before traversing queues/names/chain, matching current early layer precedence. Other fully parsed shape errors precede feature/extension admission. Static unsupported feature rejection precedes expensive actual support queries. All true/extension support failures precede reservations and command 11. There is no claim that a later host query cannot occur in a future positive-mask actual-support phase.

#### Extension admission and promotion semantics

Use a fixed owned extension registry with <=32 unique canonical names, logical IDs 0..31, minimum specVersion, immutable implementation-ready policy, parser extension number and feature-semantic dependency metadata. Compile-time check uniqueness, length/character bounds, bitwidth and dependencies. For each physical lifetime, a separately verified actual-extension enumerator publishes only a complete validated ready snapshot of actual matching names/specVersions. Retain availability bits/specVersions, not application strings. No name is admitted from Venus parser bits, raw feature Booleans or a truncated enumeration prefix. Empty and unknown actual support mean no admitted extensions.

Current registry-ready policy has zero admitted names and needs no extension transport. Future actual enumeration is a distinct codec/transport/cache increment: current rx4096 cannot represent an arbitrary full real-driver extension list. VkEnumerateDeviceExtensionProperties has no offset pagination; repeatedly requesting the same small prefix does not establish absence/presence of the rest. Before adding positive registry bits, document bounded count/fill extent, transport reply allocation, actual host result/incomplete handling and immutable physical cache publication. Never invent names from parser bit 29/287/471. This contract defines admission only, not a pretend working enumerator.

Known extension mapping: transformFeedback/geometryStreams=>VK_EXT_transform_feedback/parser 29; robustness2 fields=>VK_EXT_robustness2/parser 287; maintenance5=>VK_KHR_maintenance5/parser 471. Actual core promotions admit shaderDrawParameters from actual 1.1 and hostQueryReset from actual 1.2 without prepromotion names; aggregate11/12 require actual 1.2,aggregate13 actual 1.3. Future public API ceilings and actual API must both admit positive core semantics. Prepromotion shaderDrawParameters/hostQueryReset extension fallback remains deferred pending actual extension enumeration and a separate registry/codec contract.

Maintain semantic equivalence across aliases. When positive masks are introduced, one shared semantic bit governs aggregate11.shaderDrawParameters and standalone shaderDrawParameters; likewise aggregate12.hostQueryReset/standalone hostQueryReset. Their query publication and per-device enablement cannot disagree. Map by named fields/comptime indexes, not new magic offsets. Preserve the existing raw cache unchanged. For actual API>=aggregate availability with both raw aliases present, conservatively require both values to support a positive effective semantic; actual 1.1 uses the standalone shaderDrawParameters value. A raw mismatch cannot create a public true. The device-create forbidden-pair check still rejects both caller records; it is not a union/OR resolution. Any future change to the mismatch/error policy requires its own adopted contract/tests.

#### Smallest safe allowlist and runtime guards

The smallest truthful allowlist sufficient for all currently verified fill/copy/map/image lifecycle, storage-buffer compute/push and vertexless fixed-pipeline triangle workloads is the empty optional-feature set. These workloads create a device with no optional true feature. Preserve 55 core false and all 8 recognized-node flags false, independently of hardware support. Existing request tests that expect legacy robustBufferAccess=true success must become truthful FEATURE_NOT_PRESENT tests with no reservation/transport; they are not acceptance evidence for robust access. Do not turn that bit true just to retain an old mock expectation.

No positive bit is justified merely by a serializer, opaque SPIR-V transport, real GPU capability or one valid in-bounds shader. Positive increments need immutable command 11 enable-state proof, actual backend support, every corresponding accepted runtime state/entrypoint, shader/state metadata or proven delegated semantics, meaningful success/error/disabled tests, actual GPU behavior and zero-leak teardown. Candidates such as shaderInt64 or robustBufferAccess may be comparatively small future proofs; neither is necessary for current workloads or verified today. Their allowlist change and query projection must be atomic and documented separately.

Runtime checks consume the immutable enabled record for the parent device of each command/resource. A disabled feature rejects only operations that require it; baseline vkCmdDraw remains usable when shaderDrawParameters is false. Invalid void recording operations invalidate the command buffer before transport; creation/query APIs return documented local errors and preserve outputs. Do not derive enabled status from a physical raw snapshot, shader capability alone, requested extension strings, parser masks or another logical device. Shader-dependent positive features require inspected/proven delegated module/pipeline semantics; do not use a universal draw/dispatch gate that disables valid baseline commands. Per-device extension commands require the same stored admitted enabled bit. Cached requested/host flags do not change after creation.

#### Post-preflight encode invariant and rollback

After native preflight succeeds, choose a free cache and reserve only the real device identity first; encode before reserving its queues/rings. The owned current profile has zero modern nodes/extensions, at most sixteen queues/sixty-four priorities, and optional fifty-five false legacy words: its proven maximum command11 extent is1096 bytes within8192. Valid registry physical/device IDs are nonzero and distinct. Materialize slices solely from the owned snapshot, without rereading caller memory. If typed encoding nevertheless returns Invalid or Limit, treat it as an internal validation invariant failure: release that freshly reserved device record exactly once, return VK_ERROR_INITIALIZATION_FAILED with NULL output, and leave queue/ring/cache/enable-state/transport/loss state unchanged. No bytes have reached the backend, so no uncertain host ownership exists and sticky device loss is inappropriate. The monotonically consumed namespace identity is not rewound; a released slot may later receive a new identity. Neither encoder error denotes host-memory exhaustion. Retain the exact bounded serializer proof and native minimum/maximum accepted snapshot comparisons; do not inject a production fault variant merely to force this mathematically excluded branch. Existing resource reservation failures still return OUT_OF_HOST_MEMORY and use deterministic staged rollback. Once transport starts, preserve the separately specified uncertain-retirement rules.

#### Pinned DXVK2.7.1 Vulkan 1.3 blockers

Primary source is local pinned src/dxvk/dxvk_device_info.cpp:getFeatureList/checkDeviceCompatibility and dxvk_instance.h:DxvkVulkanApiVersion. This exact pin requires 36 core/promoted true fields, plus 3 extension true fields, pipeline_library and swapchain. It also requires 256 push bytes; current public properties clamp to 128 although tested internal maxPush support reaches 256. These requirements apply during DXVK initialization even for the black-box clear/event-query/present test; do not bypass its compatibility checks.

| Required group | Exact required fields | Present implementation boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Core raster/blend | depthBiasClamp,depthClamp,dualSrcBlend,fillModeNonSolid,independentBlend,multiViewport,sampleRateShading | Current graphics pipeline rejects enabled depth/clamp/bias/blend, non-fill polygon mode, multisampling and viewport counts!=1. |
| Core draw/query | fullDrawIndexUint32,geometryShader,multiDrawIndirect,occlusionQueryPrecise | No indexed/indirect draw/index binding/query-pool runtime; pipeline accepts exactly vertex+fragment stages. |
| Core image/shader | imageCubeArray,shaderClipDistance,shaderCullDistance,shaderImageGatherExtended,shaderInt64,shaderSampledImageArrayDynamicIndexing,textureCompressionBC | No proved per-feature enabled shaders/cube/compressed sampling; descriptor writes are buffer-only and samplers/image descriptors absent. Header-checked SPIR-V transport is not per-feature proof. |
| Core robustness | robustBufferAccess | Bounded driver bookkeeping exists, but enabled robustness and meaningful out-of-range shader behavior remain unproved. |
| 1.1 | shaderDrawParameters | Direct draw exists; DrawID/BaseVertex/BaseInstance shader semantics and indexed/indirect paths not proved. |
| 1.2 descriptors | descriptorIndexing,descriptorBindingSampledImageUpdateAfterBind,descriptorBindingUpdateUnusedWhilePending,descriptorBindingPartiallyBound,runtimeDescriptorArray | Current descriptor pool/layout/set policy rejects required flags/chains, tracks fixed initialized buffers and pending owners; no sampled-image update-after-bind/partial binding runtime. |
| 1.2 addresses/query/sync | bufferDeviceAddress,hostQueryReset,timelineSemaphore | No address getter/device-address allocation/usage support, query pools/reset or timeline counter/wait/signal API; semaphores currently binary/no chain. |
| 1.2 shader/sampler | samplerMirrorClampToEdge,uniformBufferStandardLayout,vulkanMemoryModel | No sampler creation; no enabled/actual shader proof for these layouts/memory-model semantics. |
| 1.3 | dynamicRendering,maintenance4,shaderDemoteToHelperInvocation,shaderZeroInitializeWorkgroupMemory,synchronization2 | Only legacy renderpass/barrier/submit path; no begin/end rendering, sync2 command/submit structures, maintenance4 query/runtime or modern shader proofs. |
| Required extension flags | VK_EXT_robustness2.robustBufferAccess2,nullDescriptor;VK_KHR_maintenance5.maintenance5 | Actual extension enumeration/admission absent; null buffer descriptors currently rejected; modern maintenance5 metadata/command breadth absent. |
| Required extension names | VK_KHR_pipeline_library,VK_KHR_swapchain | No pipeline-library admission/linking or Win32 surface/swapchain/acquire/present lifecycle. |

Additional initialization gaps: actual/public API 1.3 ceiling; Properties2 aggregate11/12/13/properties extensions; QueueFamilyProperties2,MemoryProperties2,FormatProperties2 and ImageFormatProperties2; accurate required limits, format/sampler/descriptor families; enabled-state-aware runtime; Win32 WSI and receiver-backed presentation/export. DXVK source explicitly calls these modern query families. Vulkan 1.3 advertisement also needs its mandatory semantic/function/property contract, beyond merely satisfying this DXVK field list. Existing four physical triangle paths and actual Win11 static/DLL/loader tests prove their current boundaries only.

#### Meaningful verification and atomic sequence

Before implementation, root adopts the parsing/admission/zero-record selection/error order and exact enable-state/scratch ABI. First integrate native preflight and immutable zero-enabled state with no advertisement change, using the separately verified owned device codec. Test every 55 legacy and 137 core+extended true request against current zero policy: FEATURE_NOT_PRESENT, no frontend/reservation/ring/cache mutation, output NULL. Include host-raw true/cold-cache cases so support cannot be inferred from mocks. All false null/legacy/Features2/canonical/reversed legal chains, unknown nodes between known nodes, multiple loader nodes,64 headers/65th unread, cycles, null/misaligned arrays, malformed Booleans/duplicates/promotion pairs/names and packet limits require exact outcomes. The owned feature parser recognizes exactly its nine admitted schemas plus loader-private headers. Every other bounded tag is treated as unknown and skipped without inspecting payload; this includes an APPLICATION_INFO tag encountered as a header. Vulkan does not define valid application use of APPLICATION_INFO in a device chain, so a regression may prove bounded skipping at this experimental driver boundary, but must not call that input a legal Vulkan application request. Valid unknown-header acceptance is the positive requirement. Do not expand a general blacklist of unrelated Vulkan structure types: that would contradict the bounded unknown-header rule and require unsupported payload inspection. Recognized feature-node malformation remains INITIALIZATION_FAILED.

Use independent pinned C command 11 expected bytes constructed from the accepted owned snapshot after legitimate unknown/zero modern node stripping. Mutate original flags/headers/names/priorities after ACK and prove enabled state and encoded values immutable. Verify multiple physical/parent/device isolation via permitted zero records and distinct ownership; no fake positive profile or production-mask fault variant. Host rejection releases all reservations; malformed identity/transport loss preserves uncertain owners; refused/failed destruction retains state; ACK destroy/abandon scrubs all state. The historical eighteen-source seam gains both venus_device_native and venus_device_wire through the native adapter import graph: twenty expected owned sources, confirmed from actual recursive imports before acceptance. Add the fresh device C oracle exactly once beside the existing render oracle; derive and verify the complete source/module/link inventory instead of reusing historical eighteen-source proof. Require >=90% real production coverage, native/C+Debug genuine ASan/LSan/C-UBSan zero leaks, ordinary full checks, exact Windows layout/crosslinks and actual Win11 fixtures. Real enabled feature GPU proof and actual DXVK remain distinct later milestones; every increment has its own atomic commit and root tracker attribution.

Implement the pure native adapter in src/vgpu/venus_device_native.zig, separate from the global ICD mutex/handles. Its documented public owned_request_t has the exact2696-byte declaration above. pub fn preflight(info_address: ?*const anyopaque) !owned_request_t borrows accessible immutable native inputs synchronously and returns only owned values, or Invalid/LayerNotPresent/FeatureNotPresent/ExtensionNotPresent mapped by the ICD to the specified Vulkan errors. It has no output application pointer, transport/query/reservation, allocation or shared state; independent calls are thread safe. The ICD separately validates/initializes the output and checks live physical/binding state before invocation. The adapter validates the entire request, rejects any requested true/name under current empty policy, and strips every valid all-false modern record. It exposes no future positive admission path or additional enabled feature. Encoding remains through the existing typed device_wire codec after the ICD materializes borrowed slices exclusively from the returned owned request; device enabled-state ownership remains in the global ICD cache. Direct malformed output/storage tests belong to the ICD integration, not this pure adapter. Every adapter/runtime declaration, imported codec and meaningful test must enter fresh coverage/access/Windows gates before public integration credit. No edits to frozen public ICD or existing physical fixtures until the complete15-case physical matrix releases its source lease.

### Bounded actual device-extension count/fill codec

#### Scope and interface boundary

Implement an allocation-free Zig codec in src/vgpu/venus_extensions_wire.zig for pinned Venus command14, vkEnumerateDeviceExtensionProperties, with no layer name. It encodes actual physical-device count/fill requests and validates replies into caller-owned values. This increment does not change public ICD enumeration, API version, admission masks, transport quotas, cache layouts or DLL/worker state. Its independent C request oracle lives in tests/vgpu/extensions_wire_oracle.c; the renderer reply oracle is tests/vgpu/extensions_reply_oracle.c. Separate translation units preserve each generated header namespace, as the existing feature query/reply oracles do; neither contains a handwritten replacement serializer. All public codec declarations have structured ownership/error/thread documentation. Runtime integration and actual host enumeration are separate atomic changes after this codec passes its own gates. Host presence alone never enables or advertises an extension; later publication intersects actual host support, negotiated parser support and verified runtime implementation.

#### Pinned format and memory layout

Primary exact references are build/venus_protocol/vn_protocol_driver_device.h's command14 encoder/decoder and vn_protocol_driver_structs.h's VkExtensionProperties encoder, backed by pinned driver/renderer generated headers. Request is44 bytes, entirely little-endian: command14 u32 at0; reply flag1 u32 at4; nonzero physical identity u64 at8; absent layer-string length0 u64 at16; count-pointer-present1 u64 at24; initial count u32 at32; properties array length u64 at36. Count query uses count0/array0. Fill uses capacity1..1024 as both count and array length; partial extension records have no payload. request_t owns bytes[44] and used=44, no native pointer.

Reply prefix is28 bytes: command14 u32 at0; signed VkResult i32 at4; count pointer presence1 u64 at8; returned count u32 at16; properties array length u64 at20. Count-only reply requires array0 and VK_SUCCESS. Fill requires array length exactly returned count, count<=requested capacity, VK_SUCCESS or VK_INCOMPLETE. Other positive results are corrupt; negative results yield Backend after valid bounded metadata, without publishing output. Count beyond1024 yields Limit in count mode rather than pretending the driver returned fewer extensions. Fill capacities outside1..1024 are Invalid before reading input. Zero returned count is valid, including incomplete fill; no unsupported assumption about driver list stability is made.

Each full entry is268 bytes: u64 array length256;256 extension-name bytes;u32 specVersion. Header and all entry extents must fit the borrowed reply. MaxReplyBytes=28+1024*268=274460; reject inputs above that bound. Existing command owners expose their entire initialized readback capacity, so accept unused trailing capacity bytes up to this bound and inspect only the exact validated command prefix/entries. Trailing bytes are not extension data. Do not require or infer a zero tail in unused capacity.

extension_t owns name[256]u8 and version:u32, exact size260/alignment4; name is normalized with one NUL and zero tail. Name must terminate within its fixed256-byte wire extent, have length4..255, start VK_, and contain only ASCII letters/digits/underscore. Case-sensitive duplicate names reject the complete fill. specVersion preserves all32 bits without inventing a version range. Vendor bytes after the first NUL are ignored and scrubbed in owned output; Vulkan does not require their initialization. No borrowed C strings, strlen, pointer chaining or allocations are used.

#### Execution, publication and errors

Public encode_count/encode_fill validate nonzero identity and fill bounds before constructing a complete owned44-byte request. Public decode_count validates prefix, mode, result and quota before returning a copied count. Public decode_fill takes a mutable caller slice of1..1024 extension_t records, disjoint from its immutable borrowed input. It first validates the complete prefix, all entry sizes/names and every duplicate without mutating any output; only then a second bounded pass copies count records and zeroes their unused name tails. Records beyond returned count remain byte-identical. Return fill_result_t containing copied count and incomplete:Boolean. Errors Invalid (local capacity), Corrupt (wire/truncation/metadata/name/duplicate/positive-result), Limit (valid count query exceeds quota), Backend (negative host result) preserve the entire output. No errors are interpreted as successful enumeration or relaxed to a partial name set. Publication requires caller-provided accessible disjoint storage and immutable input throughout both passes; arbitrary native memory accessibility is not proved. Duplicate validation compares bounded names with at most1024*1023/2 pairs; execution and temporary storage are bounded, with no global state or retained buffers.

#### Concurrency, lifetime and teardown

All values are passed/copied or borrowed synchronously; functions are thread safe for disjoint output slices and immutable inputs. No mutex, atomics, external calls, heap, owning handles or deallocation are needed. One request/decoded output has no lifetime dependency on another. Error paths allocate nothing, acquire nothing and publish nothing. Callers exclusively own requests/output records and may clear/reuse them after the call. The independent C oracles use bounded buffers and explicitly caller-owned test allocations; every allocation is released deterministically and native Windows artifacts retain exact hashes.

#### Verification and later integration

Independent pinned C request and renderer reply encoders verify literal44-byte requests, every scalar offset, signed errors, count/fill metadata and each entry. Meaningful Zig tests cover count/fill boundaries1/1024, returned zero/full/incomplete, exact names/specVersion/normalized tails, duplicate first/last, every truncated prefix/entry field, corrupt command/presence/array lengths/results/name bytes and output byte preservation. Exercise maximum274460-byte reply and all over-limit/local capacities; use std.testing.allocator with zero leaked bytes. A separate deterministic256-cycle stress test varies count0..8, successful/incomplete replies, unique name lengths4..255 and arbitrary32-bit versions. Every cycle checks normalization and untouched output tail; corrupted final entries preserve the entire prior output. Run this stress corpus through actual native decoder calls in Debug/ReleaseSafe, genuine access instrumentation and Windows. Production-only line/branch coverage must meet90%, compiler guards separately accounted. Genuine owned-function LLVM ASan with retained Debug guards/reverse proof, actual final-symbol accesses and freshly C-ASan/LSan/UBSan oracles must pass; native Windows units run with exact guest SHA and exit. No GPU/DXVK credit for codec-only acceptance.

Later actual query integration must enlarge the ICD reply owner and both TCP endpoint quotas coherently to at least274460, preserve their original finite deadlines, and validate count/fill races with a finite retry count. There is no Vulkan enumeration offset; repeated small fills cannot paginate a list. Never replace the full list with the first entries from a4096-byte reply. Compute actual private support cache/mask storage and rollback/loss rules before integration, and query real Windows-to-host devices after immutable source/runtime snapshots are established. Keep existing4096 transport and source freezes untouched in this pure increment.

### Device-import complete owned seam and preserved receipts

The public native-preflight integration adds venus_device_native.zig and venus_device_wire.zig to the historical owned graph. The exact expected embedded source names are venus_icd,venus_features_native,venus_features_wire,venus_descriptor_wire,venus_icd_profiles,venus_compute_state,venus_compute_wire,venus_render_wire,venus_graphics_wire,venus_graphics_state,venus_graphics_pipeline_wire,venus_graphics_command_wire,venus_device_native,venus_device_wire (all .zig): fourteen. The separately linked capabilities,command,objects,instance_wire,query_wire,values sources retain their six exact names: twenty total. Derive actual recursive imports and require equality to this set, not a permissive minimum. Extension wire remains standalone and unimported in this increment. Every runtime declaration/selected definition retains exact original Debug guard stores and whole-module reverse equality; every final mapped symbol exists uniquely. Existing native features_native.publish_batch absent-body reason remains the sole exact native exception; its test body still requires actual access hooks. No new lazy exception or test/std/C hook substitution.

Build fresh device_wire_oracle.c once beside each existing independent oracle. Replace any positional native/test oracle selection by an explicit validated name-to-object map so insertion cannot silently shift features query/reply references. Native public fixture, Zig allocator suite, coverage and Windows link lists use the required oracles exactly once. Preserve all historical eighteen-source objects, reports and physical matrices; never overwrite build/icd_complete_seam_safety or borrow an eighteen-source proof for the new graph.

The updated seam CLI takes a caller-exclusive base (normally build/icd_complete_seam_safety_20), creates a unique run-UTC-PID directory, and writes an immutable per-run completion record only after BOTH native and test binaries pass complete twenty-source original-IR/object/final-binary/access/zero-checker gates. The record contains canonical run path, exact source set/current hashes, and native/test report SHA256 references. Optional caller-owned latest-record publication uses a private temporary file and atomic replacement only after successful completion; old immutable run records/reports remain. A failed attempt produces no completion record and cannot report a previous successful run as that attempt's acceptance. All temporary files/descriptors/subprocesses have deterministic release; original180s tool bounds and256MiB proof bounds remain.

The physical CLI accepts mutually exclusive explicit --seam-output and --seam-record references. Record mode first validates its exact twenty-source/current-source identity and both report hashes, then borrows that canonical immutable run directory. Direct-path mode applies the same exact source/object/IR/proof validity, never a guessed latest directory. Its existing native reproof, three final executable/DSO access proofs, unchanged driver policy, session cleanup, original workload/fence deadlines and full fifteen-case matrix remain required. New physical output uses its own unique base/run directory; current eighteen-source receipts stay byte-identical. All source/header/runtime/object/report hashes are checked before and after acceptance.

Aggregate physical gating depends on a fresh successful complete-seam target in the SAME make dependency graph, then consumes only its atomically published record. A failed prerequisite stops make before physical launch, even if an older latest record exists. Serialize shared receiver/GPU ownership as before. Root integrates aggregate safety/CI ordering separately after candidate checks; embedded-only IR proof is superseded by the complete same-binary seam rather than counted as another independent module acceptance. Require actual new native/Windows and hardware proof before credit; twenty-source instrumentation alone does not establish Windows/DXVK/API feature support.

### CI parent budget for the complete owned seam

The Linux transport job has a60-minute parent timeout. The successful clean
checkpoint c2faa4c, run37557528193/job112587097749, consumed2496s
(2026-10-07T01:31:31Z through02:13:07Z), before the new device-native,
device-wire, extension-wire and complete20-source seam gates. The retained
receipt is build/ci_c2faa4c_verified.json. Parent budget accommodates those
additional proofs; individual proof children retain180s/256MiB bounds, physical
GPU cases retain300s, and GPU fences retain their1s deadlines. Any failed gate
fails CI; parent timeout grants no acceptance or retry override. Windows remains
15minutes until measured new native-gate execution warrants a separate change.

### Full extension reply prerequisite: bounded byte transfer, unchanged TCP profile

The allocation-free byte-transfer owner is implemented and independently gated.
Current ICD rx=4096, service reply allocation=65536 and TCP operation reply
limit=4096 remain unchanged; no large ICD query is enabled. The pure extension codec permits1024 native entries
and274460 reply bytes; command14 enumeration has no record-offset argument, so
repeating smaller fills cannot enumerate later records. The already implemented
RequestReply envelope instead has a byte offset (argument_zero) and length
(argument_one). Dispatch forwards both to venus_receiver_reply; receiver bounds
check against the actual mapped allocation. TCP retains offsets and limits each
length to4096. Therefore future assembly reads one completed immutable reply in
successive byte ranges, never paginates extension records or changes TCPv1.

The existing command owner retains its submission/fence/response machinery,
adding an owned size_t reply_offset and CommandReading=4; earlier enum values
remain0..3. Its measured x64 native ABI is80 bytes/alignment8, cursor48,
CPU fence56, command ID64, phase68 and sticky status72. Every native consumer
must rebuild together; no owner structure is a wire/shared-memory record.
ICD unbind refuses Submitted/Reading/Ready even when no Vulkan objects remain;
regressions require exact command, profile and namespace preservation. Initialization
requires the whole zero record and sets offset0. Start publishes the existing
reply-stream prefix with that owner's entire rx extent and accepts one nonzero
CPU fence; only Idle may start. Poll in Submitted performs the existing CPU poll;
Again retains Submitted/offset0. A validated successful CPU poll enters Reading.
Each Reading poll requests at most one min(4096,rx_bytes-reply_offset) byte
range at exactly reply_offset, into the exclusive rx suffix. A validated success
alone advances the cursor by that length. If bytes remain, return Again in
Reading with the advanced cursor, so the caller controls the next operation.
Frontend Again retains the same cursor/phase and retries the same range; partial
private bytes are never available through take. Every other failed read after
accepted submission becomes existing sticky Lost, including Invalid/Limit; no
new start, result publication or silent reset is permitted. After the last
successful range, validate the first reply command identity before entering
Ready. Take returns the entire borrowed rx only from Ready, then clears offset,
fence and command ID back to Idle. Submitted/Reading take returns Again with
NULL/zero outputs. Free still requires actual pending receiver retirement or
abandonment before releasing borrowed buffers. Sole session thread owns all
cursor mutations; no pointer retention beyond initialized borrowed storage.

Current rx<=4096 must preserve one-poll/one-read success and all existing status
precedence. Tests must inject Again at first/middle/final chunks, each malformed
response field, terminal statuses, wrong final identity and caller start/take
while Reading. Test exact4/4096/4100/274460 and16MiB supported owner extents,
check every byte and request offset/length, protect adjacent canaries, and prove
no cursor advance on unsuccessful reads. Preserve≥90% production coverage,
independent C ABI checks, genuine owned access hooks, zero allocator/leak gates
and actual Windows execution. The original per-call frontend deadlines remain.
The current ICD transact loop has1000 poll iterations separated by1ms sleeps;
it is an iteration bound, not an elapsed-time whole-command deadline. A274460-byte
reply requires68 successful reads (67*4096 plus28); a16MiB owner requires4096.
Standalone callers must drive that many bounded polls. Future large ICD-query
integration must specify a monotonic whole-query deadline and pass the remaining
budget to a supported frontend mechanism before coding; per-operation deadlines
alone cannot establish that guarantee. No large operation is added now.

Capabilities160 does not report actual reply allocation. A larger guest owner
must never infer allocation from a parser mask or unchanged capset. Before any
full extension query, separately implement and verify an explicit trusted host
configuration whose requested524288-byte power-of-two receiver resource is
actually mapped at exactly524288. The current worker CLI initializes its service
configuration internally; controller private JSON cannot set reply_bytes. The
configuration mechanism, compatibility/error handling and actual mapping proof
require their own atomic contract and gates. Keep TCP command/reply ceilings and
wire profile unchanged. Fail closed on insufficient actual host capacity.

A future extension query may use a separately initialized exclusive command
owner sharing the idle binding's tx under the existing ICD mutex, with bounded
private fill rx; it must propagate sticky transport uncertainty to the binding
and retain borrowed storage until retirement. This integration and per-physical
cache allocation/lifecycle need a separate specification before coding. Public
extensions and optional features remain empty/false until the guest runtime
allowlist, actual host list and enabled device state all support their semantics.

8192/274460/16MiB extents, every byte/canary, first/middle/final Again,6 terminal
